# Replacing missing values with most fequent

MostFrequentEntry = df['Cuisines'].value_counts().idxmax()
import numpy as np
df['Cuisines'].replace(np.nan,MostFrequentEntry, inplace=True)
print(df.isnull().sum())

restaurant_name = 'Le Petit Souffle'
def recommend_restaurants(restaurant_name, df, similarity_matrix):
    idx = df[df['Restaurant Name'] == restaurant_name].index[0]

## Recommending restaurants based of cuisines

for cuisine in df['Cuisines'].unique()[:5]:
    restaurant = df[df['Cuisines'] == cuisine].iloc[0]['Restaurant Name']
    result = recommend_restaurants(restaurant, df, similarity_matrix)
    print(f"\nRecommendations for {cuisine}:")
    print(result)

In [62]:
for cuisine in df['Cuisines'].unique()[:5]:  
    restaurant_row = df[df['Cuisines'].str.contains(cuisine, na=False, case=False)]
    
    if restaurant_row.empty:
        print(f"\nNo restaurants found for cuisine: {cuisine}")
        continue
        restaurant = restaurant_row.iloc[0]['Restaurant Name']
    result = recommend_restaurants(restaurant, df, similarity_matrix)
    
    print(f"\nRecommendations for {cuisine}:")
    print(result)
